### [1] [Signal-Driven Pervasive Game Design: The LifeSync-Games Framework as a Player Experience Integration Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03169)
*J. Macías-Cáceres,F. Gutiérrez-Vela,P. Paderewski-Rodriguez,R. González-Ibáñez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出LifeSync-Games（LSG）框架，将玩家的生理和认知状态（体力活动、睡眠质量、记忆、决策速度）作为横贯性集成层（PEIL），贯穿于普适游戏原有的空间、时间、社会三个维度，并设计了游戏化集成门户及五项HCI设计原则，目前处于设计阶段，尚未开展数据收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有普适游戏虽扩展了"魔法圈"的空间、时间和社会维度，但将玩家的生理与认知状态仅视为被动接收者，未作为主动信号参与游戏设计。作者希望突破这一局限，将玩家状态信号提升为驱动游戏响应的核心机制。

Method: 提出Player Experience Integration Layer (PEIL)作为横向贯穿三个普适维度的集成层；设计LSG门户作为游戏化集成工件，通过可兑换点数、现实任务和结构性游戏化调解现实与虚拟的交换；基于自我决定理论与心流理论提出五项HCI设计原则，并实例化于六款商业游戏中；设计了包含70-80名参与者的准实验验证协议。

Result: 当前报告的是LSG的设计阶段成果，规则阈值与门户参数属于待经验校准的设计决策，尚未进行数据收集。主要成果为理论扎实的框架与验证协议，以及一项已规划的准实验研究方案。

Conclusion: 本研究的核心贡献在于提供了一个理论驱动、面向玩家敏感集成的框架及验证方案，使普适游戏能够真正响应玩家在实际生活中的生物学与认知状态，为后续实证校准和验证奠定了设计基础。

Abstract: Pervasive games extend the magic circle across spatial, temporal, and social dimensions, yet treat the player's physiological and cognitive state as a passive receptor rather than an active signal. This paper presents LifeSync-Games (LSG), a framework that (unlike proposals treating player signals as an additional dimension), operationalizes them as a Player Experience Integration Layer (PEIL) acting transversally across the three existing pervasive dimensions through verified real-world signals: physical activity, sleep quality, memory, and decision speed. The framework introduces a gamified integration artifact (the LSG portal) that mediates the real <-> virtual exchange through redeemable points, real-world missions, and structural gamification. Five HCI design principles grounded in Self-Determination Theory and Flow Theory are proposed, instantiated across six commercial video games, together with a study protocol (n = 70-80 participants, quasi-experimental design). This paper reports the design stage of LSG: rule thresholds and portal parameters are design decisions pending empirical calibration; no data collection has yet been conducted. The main contribution is a theoretically grounded framework and validation protocol positioning player-sensitive integration as the mechanism enabling pervasive games to respond to the player's actual biological and cognitive state.

</details>


### [2] [Code Black: Desktop-Mediated Co-Design of AR-HMD Microinteractions for Emergency Department Teamwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03295)
*Jonathan Segal,Jalynn Nicoly,Francisco Ortega,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过推测性共同设计方法，与12名医护工作者使用桌面媒介的Unity 3D设计探针，探索AR头戴显示器在急诊科团队协作中的空间用户界面需求，最终提出SCF-HMD框架和视觉设计目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科团队在高度不确定性下协调角色、药物决策和时间关键干预，AR-HMD有潜力通过空间锚定信息支持团队协作，但缺乏对空间界面如何支持此类协作的深入理解。

Method: 采用推测性共同设计研究，使用可编辑的桌面媒介Unity 3D设计探针，让12名医护工作者可视化并细化基于角色的通知、任务定时器和剂量验证等设想AR-HMD界面；另有5名返回参与者和26名额外医护工作者提供后续反馈。

Result: 研究发现桌面媒介空间共同设计引发了关于角色可见性、任务关联计时和验证导向剂量辅助的规范性规格，同时揭示了杂波、共享意识、沟通、隐私和可靠性等方面的张力。

Conclusion: 研究贡献了SCF-HMD框架和视觉设计目录，用于将专家对工作想象概念的批判性反馈转化为未来AR-HMD系统的情境化目标，而非评估功能性系统或团队临床绩效。

Abstract: Emergency Department (ED) teams coordinate shifting roles, medication decisions, and time-critical interventions under uncertainty. Augmented reality head-mounted displays (AR-HMDs) have shown potential to spatially anchor information during care, creating opportunities to examine how spatial interfaces might support teamwork. We conducted a speculative co-design study with 12 healthcare workers (HCWs) using an editable, desktop-mediated Unity-based 3D design probe to visualize and refine work-as-imagined AR-HMD interfaces for role-based notifications, task-specific timers, and dosage verification. Guided by microinteraction rules, participants identified future spatial user interfaces (SUI) requirements such as how they appear, update, or are dismissed in relation to clinical practice, safety concerns, and existing tools. Five returning participants and 26 additional HCWs subsequently provided follow-up feedback on derived visual interface alternatives. Findings show that desktop-mediated spatial co-design elicited formative specifications for role visibility, task-linked timing, and verification-oriented dosage assistance, while revealing tensions involving clutter, shared awareness, communication, privacy, and reliability. Rather than evaluating a functional AR-HMD system or team-based clinical performance, this study contributes the Speculative Co-Design Framework for AR-HMD Teamwork (SCF-HMD) and a visual design catalog for translating expert critique of work-as-imagined (WAI) concepts into situated goals for future AR-HMD systems.

</details>


### [3] [Point&Spawn: Mid-Air Reference-Free Object Instantiation Using Gaze and Hand Gestures in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03661)
*Jihyeon Lee,Ken Pfeuffer,Jinwook Kim,Jeongmi Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Point&Spawn，一个用于XR中空中物体实例化的分阶段管线，通过方向设定、深度设定和位置精化三个步骤，在连续手势流中完成预实例化位置指定。用户研究（N=24）评估了六种免控制器技术组合，结果表明Relative Gain和Drag&Hold在速度、精度、工作量和用户体验方面优于Ray Intersection。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR中空中物体实例化需要用户在缺乏空间参考（如表面或现有物体）的情况下指定3D位置，这是一个具有挑战性的交互问题。现有研究缺乏对预实例化位置指定技术的系统评估，需要对方向设定和深度控制方法进行实证比较研究。

Method: 采用分阶段管线设计，将位置指定分解为方向设定、深度设定和位置精化三个阶段。方向设定使用Gaze或非惯用手（NDH）两种方式，深度设定采用Ray Intersection、Relative Gain和Drag&Hold三种方法，组合成六种免控制器手势技术，并在近距离和远距离两种生成深度下进行N=24的用户研究评估。

Result: Relative Gain和Drag&Hold在生成速度和精度上优于Ray Intersection，同时工作负荷更低、可用性更高且用户偏好更强。肩部参考的NDH射线提高了速度和粗精度，而基于视点的Gaze射线减少了手部移动量并保持了相当的最终精度。更远的生成深度带来了更大的时间成本，且Ray Intersection在更远深度下出现Gaze和精度成本增加的问题。

Conclusion: 研究为XR中生成操作的方向和深度控制设计提供了实证指导。Relative Gain和Drag&Hold是更优的深度设定方法，方向设定方式应根据任务需求在速度/精度与手部移动量之间权衡选择。

Abstract: Mid-air object instantiation in XR requires users to specify a 3D position without spatial references, such as surfaces or existing objects. We present Point&Spawn, a staged pipeline for pre-instantiation position specification through Direction Setting, Depth Setting, and Position Refinement within a continuous gesture flow. We evaluated six controller-free techniques combining Gaze or Non-Dominant Hand (NDH) direction setting with Ray Intersection, Relative Gain, or Drag&Hold depth setting in a user study (N=24) across Near and Far spawn depths. Relative Gain and Drag&Hold yielded faster and more accurate spawning, lower workload, higher usability, and greater preference than Ray Intersection. The shoulder-referenced NDH ray improved speed and coarse accuracy, whereas the viewpoint-based Gaze ray reduced hand movement with comparable final accuracy. Farther spawn depth imposed greater temporal costs as well as Gaze and accuracy costs with Ray Intersection. These findings offer empirical guidance for designing direction and depth control in spawning in XR.

</details>


### [4] [PlanePivoting: Exploration and Optimization of Gaze-Mouse Cursor Alignment for Spatial Object Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03665)
*Jinwook Kim,Sangmin Park,Jihyeon Lee,Sang Ho Yoon,Jeongmi Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanePivoting，一种利用视线-鼠标对齐将二维鼠标输入扩展到三维空间的多模态交互技术，通过动态调节平移平面消除二维与三维输入模式切换的需求，用户研究证明其效率优于传统3D Gizmo界面且保持相当的精度和更高的满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR平台日趋成熟，但2D和3D输入模态之间的不连贯仍是阻碍无缝工作流的关键障碍。频繁在鼠标（2D精确操作）和手势（3D操控）之间切换会导致显著的生理疲劳和认知负荷。

Method: 提出PlanePivoting多模态交互技术，利用视线与鼠标光标的叠加关系动态调制平移平面，从而无需物理切换输入模态。通过用户研究比较PlanePivoting与标准3D Gizmo界面，涵盖两种平移映射配置和两种视线光标孔径设置。

Result: PlanePivoting在效率指标上优于Gizmo界面，同时保持了相当的精确度，并获得了更高的主观满意度。

Conclusion: 视线-鼠标对齐在实现2D与3D环境间高效空间操控方面具有潜力，为XR平台中统一输入方式提供了新的设计方向。

Abstract: As XR matures into a ubiquitous computing platform, the disconnect between 2D and 3D input modalities remains a critical barrier to seamless workflow. Frequent transitions between the mouse for 2D precision and hand gestures for 3D manipulation induce significant physical fatigue and cognitive load. To address this, we introduce PlanePivoting, a multimodal interaction technique that extends standard mouse input into 3D space by leveraging gaze-mouse alignment. This technique dynamically modulates the translation plane based on the spatial overlap between the gaze and mouse cursor, eliminating the need for physical input modality switching. To systematically explore the foundational design space of gaze-mouse coordination and optimize key variables, we conducted a user study comparing PlanePivoting with a standard 3D Gizmo interface across two translation mapping profiles and two gaze cursor apertures. Results demonstrate that PlanePivoting outperforms the Gizmo on efficiency metrics while maintaining comparable precision and yielding higher subjective satisfaction. This study demonstrates the potential of gaze-mouse alignment for efficient spatial manipulation between 2D and 3D environments.

</details>


### [5] [Exploratory Unstructured Data Analysis: A Formative Study and Implications for Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03678)
*Johannes Eschner,Dominik Eitler,Max Irendorfer,Patrick Kramml,Matthias Zeppelzauer,Manuela Waldner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于大规模非结构化数据探索性分析（EluDA）的概念框架，结合了经典数据查询与可视化和主动知识构建。通过形成性研究和CLIP模型评估，发现了用户在探索过程中通过自底向上的分面分类进行概念化的行为模式，并识别出四个人机协作的关键机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模非结构化数据的探索性分析缺乏系统性的概念框架指导，且对用户在探索过程中的概念化行为理解不足，需要探索人类与AI在该场景中的协作模式。

Method: 采用形成性研究让用户对图像数据集进行概念化，同时评估CLIP模型在零样本分类和语义分组中的表现，基于研究发现提出EluDA概念框架及人机协作机会。

Result: 发现用户主要通过自底向上构建分面分类来进行概念化，很少创建有意义的空间分类；CLIP在将用户定义概念分配给图像时不可靠，但能支持语义分组。

Conclusion: 提出了四个人机协作的关键方向：智能采样与可视化实现数据可见性最大化；增量和少样本学习降低可靠分配的努力成本；自动类别、概念和分面建议；以及有效的信任校准方法。

Abstract: We propose a conceptual framework for exploratory data analysis of (large) unstructured data (EluDA), combining classical elements (querying, visualization) with active knowledge construction in the "search for structure". In a formative study, users conceptualized a structure for an image dataset during exploration. We found that users conceptualize by building faceted classifications bottom-up and rarely create meaningful spatial categorization during this process. We also evaluated CLIP for zero-shot assignment and semantic categorization, finding that it remains unreliable for assigning user-defined concepts to images but does support semantic grouping. Based on these findings, we identify and discuss four key opportunities for human-AI collaboration in EluDA: intelligent sampling and visualization to maximize data visibility; incremental and few-shot learning to minimize effort for reliable assignment; automatic category, concept, and facet suggestions to reduce effort during the search for structure; and the necessity for effective trust calibration methods.

</details>


### [6] [Auditing Contextual Bias in Human Ball-Strike Calls Using KBO's Automated Umpiring Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03786)
*Kichang Lee,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文利用韩国棒球组织（KBO）引入自动判定好球系统（ABS）的机会，审核人类裁判在好球-坏球判罚中是否存在情境性偏差。通过2021年至2026赛季的投球级数据建模，发现人类裁判在0-2球数时少判好球（-17.17个百分点效应），在3-0球数时多判好球（+6.61个百分点效应），且早期局数判罚更宽松、后期得分胶着时更严格；而ABS系统中这些效应趋近于零。其他怀疑的偏差（薪资声誉、捕手身份、主场优势）证据较弱或局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期存在关于棒球裁判好球判罚是否存在情境性偏差的争论，但缺乏一个能区分"真实偏差"与"噪声或统计假象"的基准。KBO引入ABS系统提供了一个天然的审计场景——用机器判罚作为无偏差基准，来对照人类裁判在不同比赛情境下的系统性判罚偏移。

Method: 使用KBO 2021至2026赛季的投球级数据，对靠近好球带边界的被接住投球建立"判为好球概率"模型。以2022-2023年作为人类裁判基线期，2024年及以后的ABS赛季作为诊断基准。分析维度涵盖球数（count）、比赛进度（局数及比分胶着度）、薪资声誉、捕手身份及主场背景，并采用假发现率（FDR）校正进行多重检验。

Result: （1）球数压力效应最强：0-2球数下好球率下降17.17个百分点，3-0球数下上升6.61个百分点；ABS下效应接近零。（2）比赛进度：早期局判罚偏松、第7-9局以上（尤其结束阶段胶着时）偏严；ABS期基本持平。（3）薪资声誉证据存疑且因代理指标而异。（4）捕手身份在人类时期存在异质性残差，ABS下消失。（5）主场偏差在裁判层面基本为空结果。

Conclusion: 人类裁判并非在所有维度都存在系统偏差，但好球带的判罚确实对球数压力和比赛紧张程度最为敏感。研究系统性地绘制了人类好球带情境敏感性的"地图"，厘清了哪些常见怀疑得到实证支持、哪些证据薄弱，为今后裁判偏差研究提供了方法论的审计范式。

Abstract: This paper uses the Korean Baseball Organization's adoption of the Automated Ball-Strike (ABS) system to audit long-standing claims about contextual bias in human ball-strike calls. Using pitch-level KBO data from 2021 through the available portion of the 2026 season, we model called-strike probability for taken pitches near the strike-zone boundary, with 2022-2023 as the primary human-umpire baseline and ABS seasons (2024 and onward) as a diagnostic benchmark. The strongest evidence concerns count pressure. Relative to 0--0 counts, human umpires called substantially fewer strikes in two-strike counts and more strikes in hitter-ahead three-ball counts. Specifically, in the main 0.25-ft boundary band, 0--2 was associated with a -17.17 percentage-point effect and 3--0 with a +6.61 percentage-point effect. Under ABS, the corresponding effects were close to zero and did not survive false-discovery-rate correction. Game progression shows a smaller but coherent pattern as human calls were less strike-prone in early innings and more strike-prone in innings 7--9+, especially in late-close situations, while complete ABS seasons were essentially flat. Other suspected biases are weaker or more localized. Salary-based reputation proxies provide suggestive but proxy-sensitive evidence, and catcher identity shows human-period residual heterogeneity that disappears under ABS. Home-context evidence is mostly null at the umpire level, with one FDR-significant human-period exception and an exploratory umpire-team gap best treated as an audit lead. Overall, the results do not show that human umpires were biased everywhere. Instead, they map where the human strike zone was most context-sensitive, where evidence was weaker, and where common suspicions received little support.

</details>


### [7] [GazeFS: Target-Centered Gaze-Trajectory Forecasting and Stabilization from Gaze-Head History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03868)
*Yaozheng Xia,Zaiping Zhu,Bo Pang,Minghao Xie,Hui Li,Shaorong Wang,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出GazeFS，一种基于在线目标中心视线轨迹预测与稳定的方法，通过可变长度注视-头部历史映射到下一目标中心方向和短视距搜索/聚焦估计，无需推理时目标信息。在30名参与者7960次采集片段中，聚焦偏差、片段内离散度和P90目标误差分别降低0.182°、0.257°和0.400°，置信区间排除零，且无端点回放保留了聚焦优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标中心的注视交互不仅需要抑制帧间波动，目标获取过程会产生任务对齐的注视-头部动态变化，且注视痕迹可能保留持久的目标相对残差方向，因此需要更有效的注视校正方法。

Method: 将注视校正形式化为在线目标中心注视轨迹预测与稳定，提出GazeFS框架，将可变长度的注视-头部历史映射到下一个目标中心方向和短视距搜索/聚焦阶段估计，推理时不需要目标信息。采用30名参与者五折分组交叉验证协议，结合质量控制和持续时间匹配等评估手段。

Result: 在7960个采集片段中，搜索与聚焦阶段差异在质量控制下保持稳定；聚焦偏差、片段内离散度和P90目标误差相对原始数据分别降低0.182°、0.257°和0.400°，参与者自助95%置信区间排除零；无端点回放获得原始网络相位平衡准确率0.925和AUPRC 0.993；近期历史贡献超过显式进度元数据。

Conclusion: GazeFS能有效改善聚焦目标居中性和经验残余收缩，同时将时间平滑度作为独立目标保留。框架无需推理时目标信息即可在实际应用中发挥作用。

Abstract: Target-centered gaze interaction requires more than suppressing frame-to-frame fluctuations: target acquisition produces task-aligned changes in gaze-head dynamics, while a gaze trace may retain a persistent target-relative residual direction. We formulate gaze correction as online target-centered gaze-trajectory forecasting and stabilization and introduce GazeFS, which maps a variable-length gaze-head history to the next target-center direction and a short-horizon Search/Focus estimate without target information at inference. Across 7,960 acquisition episodes from 30 participants, Search-Focus differences remain stable under quality control, onset exclusion, and duration matching. History windows improve phase decoding over the current endpoint, but explicit task progress remains a strong control. Under the 30-participant, five-fold grouped out-of-fold protocol across three seeds, the reductions relative to raw hold in Focus episode bias, within-episode dispersion, and P90 target error are 0.182 degrees, 0.257 degrees, and 0.400 degrees, with participant-bootstrap 95% confidence intervals excluding zero. Endpoint-free replay from empty history preserves the Focus advantage and yields raw-network phase balanced accuracy/AUPRC of 0.925/0.993; coordinate controls further show that recent history contributes beyond explicit progress metadata. GazeFS therefore improves Focus target centering and empirical residual contraction while leaving temporal smoothness as a separate objective.

</details>


### [8] [From Misconceptions to Evidence: What Science Teachers Make Visible When Co-Designing Agentic Learning Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03917)
*Nizam Kadir,Wei Ting Liow,Sumbul Khan,Lay Kee Ang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨科学教师如何将学科认识论工作（如诊断误解、解释证据）转译为智能体学习应用的规格说明，通过对教师专业发展工作坊中四件人工制品的质性跨案例分析，提出了一个五问设计协议（问题、学习者互动、证据、教师权威、保障）来帮助教师将科学学习需求转化为可问责的人机协作安排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学教育者日益面对能生成内容的AI工具，但学科教学依赖于引发学习者模型、诊断误解、解释证据和保持专业判断。研究追问：科学教师如何将这些认识论工作转化为智能体学习应用的规格说明？这关乎教师专业学习中的教学设计推理。

Method: 采用有界定性跨案例分析，对教师专业学习工作坊中产生的四件去标识化人工制品进行编码分析：实验设计诊断工具、动力学粒子理论对话指南、化学先前知识检测器、物理应用/脚手架工具。每件制品按学科问题、学习者互动、可视化证据、教师权威和保障五个维度编码。

Result: 四件制品均将科学学习问题与互动及教学可解释的证据相联系（误解与缺口、进行中的解释、班级层面准备模式或调查表现），但仅有两件明确表达了教师控制或评价，仅两件命名了保障措施。提案将AI定位为引导者、脚手架和证据返回机制，而非答案生成器，但在决策权和保护措施的规格说明上存在不均衡。

Conclusion: 教师专业学习应将AI应用构思视为认识论规格说明工作。五问设计协议有助于教师在构建或采用工具前，将科学学习需求转化为可问责的人机安排。

Abstract: Science educators increasingly encounter AI tools that generate content, yet disciplinary teaching depends on eliciting learners' models, diagnosing misconceptions, interpreting evidence, and preserving professional judgment. This study asks how science teachers translate such epistemic work into specifications for agentic learning applications. It contributes to the conference theme, "Innovating Pedagogies, Inspiring Minds: Transforming Science Learning," and the Teachers' Professional Learning strand by examining app co-design as a form of pedagogical reasoning. We conducted a bounded qualitative cross-case analysis of four de-identified artifacts produced in a teacher professional-learning workshop: an experimental-design diagnostic, a Kinetic Particle Theory dialogue guide, a chemistry prior-knowledge checker, and a physics application/scaffolding tool. Each artifact was coded for the disciplinary problem, learner interaction, evidence made visible, teacher authority, and safeguard.
  All four connected a science-learning problem to an interaction and pedagogically interpretable evidence: misconceptions and gaps, explanations-in-progress, class-level readiness patterns, or investigation performance. However, only two made teacher control or evaluation explicit, and only two named a safeguard. The proposals therefore positioned AI less as an answer generator than as an elicitor, scaffold, and evidence-return mechanism, while leaving decision rights and protections unevenly specified.
  We argue that teacher professional learning should treat AI app ideation as epistemic specification work. A five-question design protocol--problem, learner interaction, evidence, teacher authority, and safeguard--can help teachers transform science-learning needs into accountable human-AI arrangements before building or adopting a tool.

</details>

*Xianni Wang,Javier Romero Davila,Saku Sourulahti,Torsten Schaub,Jussi P. P. Jokinen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于回答集编程（ASP）的方法，自动生成并优化UI布局，在满足网格对齐、分组、色彩和谐、留白等设计目标及设计师偏好约束的同时平衡功能与美学，并通过三项研究验证了其有效性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建视觉美观的用户界面通常需要大量手动迭代，效率低下且成本高，亟需一种能够自动生成和优化UI布局的方法。

Method: 采用回答集编程（ASP）对元素属性和相对定位的约束进行编码，自动生成满足设计目标（如网格对齐、分组、色彩和谐、留白）与设计师指定偏好的布局方案。

Result: 在三项研究中，两项用户研究显示参与者对完整ASP模型生成布局的评分高于随机设计和基于简单启发式的设计；设计师反馈ASP生成的布局有利于早期草图绘制和探索。

Conclusion: ASP方法能有效自动生成高质量UI布局，在功能与美学之间取得平衡，并可作为设计师早期设计阶段的有力辅助工具。

Abstract: Creating visually appealing user interfaces often requires extensive manual iteration. We propose an approach that applies answer set programming (ASP) to automatically generate and optimize UI layouts while satisfying design objectives such as grid alignment, grouping, color harmony, and whitespace, along with designer-specified preferences. Our method encodes constraints on element properties and relative positioning, producing layouts that balance functional and aesthetic goals. We evaluate this approach in three studies. Across two user studies, participants rated layouts generated with the full ASP model higher than both random designs and those based on simple heuristics. Designers reported that ASP-generated layouts supported early-stage sketching and exploration.

</details>

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Abstract: Declarative UI protocols such as A2UI let applications generate interactive UIs by selecting pre-built components from a catalog and binding their props to application data, rather than emitting frontend code from scratch. This contract is attractive for production systems because of safety and consistency. An open question is: can low-latency and low-cost small models achieve the required quality for A2UI-based UI generation? To answer this, we systematically study three controllable design choices for catalog-conditioned A2UI generation: supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data construction method, model size, and component-catalog size. Across two React/TypeScript domains and four base checkpoints spanning two model families (Qwen 3.5 0.8B/2B/4B; SmolLM 3B), we find: (i) a 4B fine-tuned student recovers ~98% of teacher semantic quality and ~97% of teacher visual quality at more than an order of magnitude lower cost than frontier API calls; (ii) both augmented strategies (Perturbed-catalog and Constrained-GT) Pareto-dominate the unaugmented Full-catalog baseline, while specializing on different axes; (iii) even small models can handle and benefit from relatively large component catalog size. We distill these results into practitioner-facing trade-offs and deployment recommendations across the three design choices.

</details>

Motivation: 传统应用英语教材以固定的纸质序列呈现，无法满足学习者个性化需求和实时诊断反馈，而人工智能技术的进步为教材形态从静态向自适应学习系统转变提供了可能。

Method: 提出五层架构的AI驱动实用英语教材系统，包括知识映射、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排和教师端治理五个层次，并通过原型系统对186名非英语专业本科生进行为期8周的教学实验验证。

Result: 相比静态数字教材，AI驱动系统的单元完成准确率从72.4%提升到84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少了31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的英语教材能够在维持课程稳定性基础上提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料和可追踪的课堂数据，有效提升教学效果并减轻教师负担。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [12] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"治理型"企业分析方法：语言模型仅负责解析问题意图，而由确定性策略选择并执行预先批准的分析程序。在440次运行中，三个8B模型进行运行时规划（SQL生成和工具选择）均未完全匹配"答案+证据"合同，而策略执行的方案在110/110次中完全达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析场景需要兼顾表达力和合规性。直接让语言模型在运行时自由规划分析任务虽然灵活，但难以保证结果的可追溯性、一致性和合规性。作者希望探索一种受管控的分析范式，在限定分析类别内保持表达力的同时，确保结果可重放（replayable）且满足答案与证据的双重要求。

Method: 采用"治理型"架构：语言模型仅负责任何意图理解（如Qwen3-8B），确定性策略负责选择并执行预批准的分析程序。分析类别涵盖关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口函数、排名和相似性等操作。对比实验包括三个8B模型的运行时规划方案（动态生成SQL并选择性工具）与策略执行方案，共进行440次运行。

Result: 330次运行时规划的任务全部未能跨所有测试数据集匹配完整的"答案+证据"合同；而策略执行的方案在110次测试中全部成功匹配。研究强调这是特定配置下的结果，并不代表运行时代理在其他设计下无法成功。

Conclusion: 在企业分析场景中，通过"固定含义、策略、数据和执行规则"的治理式方法，能够在限定分析类别内保持表达力并确保结果可重放。该方法在答案与证据的完整性上显著优于当前配置下的运行时规划方案，但结论受限于特定配置，需进一步探索其他设计空间。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [13] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Macro Commit（SMC），一种用于双层Agent系统的运行时机制：大型权威Actor模型生成官方轨迹，而快速推测性Drafter模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测并预执行未来动作链。SMC从训练轨迹挖掘重复的多动作骨架存入宏库，运行时用于匹配Drafter预测的动作链，当Actor的下一个工具调用与预执行的首个动作匹配时，SMC将其余预执行步骤连同观测提交到官方轨迹。实验表明SMC在保持精度的同时显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的LLM Agent系统在串行执行的动作-观测轮次中浪费大量墙钟时间，不仅花在模型推理上，每次工具调用、环境转换和观测都会延迟后续决策，单步推测性动作的加速有限，需要更高效的推测执行机制。

Method: SMC采用双层Agent架构：大型权威Actor模型产生官方轨迹，快速Drafter模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测并预执行未来动作链。通过从训练轨迹挖掘重复的多动作骨架（macro）存入库中，运行时将其与Drafter预测的动怍链进行匹配；当Actor的下一个工具调用与预执行的首个动作匹配时，SMC将剩余预执行步骤及其观测一并提交至官方轨迹。

Result: 使用Qwen3.5-27B INT4作为权威Actor和Qwen3.5-4B作为推测性Drafter时，SMC在τ²-Bench Telecom子集上与顺序Agent准确率持平，相比Speculative Actions（SA）基线降低10.23%延迟，相比顺序执行降低18.59%；在AppWorld上相比SA基线降低7.7%墙钟时间，相比顺序执行降低44.9%，任务完成度仅有小幅下降。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的多步推测性执行重用方案，能超越单步推测性动作的局限进一步降低Agent延迟，同时保持整体准确率，代码已公开。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [14] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFence协议，解决分布式LLM-agent团队中"过时计划执行"问题——即状态虽新鲜但计划已失效。该协议通过依赖范围的行动验证，让计划引用精确的公共记录，执行器仅验证可能影响待执行外部操作的记录。实验表明，在30个受控实时工作流中，PlanFence全部任务完成且无无效操作，而freshness-only执行器在每项任务中都执行了过时计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM-agent团队即使读到最新的共享事实，仍可能基于过时的计划行动。现有研究过度关注状态新鲜度，忽视了"计划授权行动有效性"这一独立维度。状态新鲜并不保证计划仍然有效，这构成了实际部署中的安全隐患。

Method: 引入PlanFence，一种依赖范围的行动验证协议。计划引用其使用过的精确公共记录，执行器仅验证能影响待执行外部操作的记录。验证不完整时进行重规划或阻塞。

Result: 30个受控实时工作流中，freshness-only执行器在每项任务中均执行了过时计划，而PlanFence全部完成且无无效操作。受控回放揭示两个条件边界：低变更率下主动同步降低协调停滞；PlanFence在高变更率和共享键空间增长时避免重复更新路径协调和无关状态验证。

Conclusion: PlanFence在受控环境中提供了安全性和系统成本优势，但这些是受控的安全性和系统开销结果，并非通用任务准确率提升。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [15] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于提示工程的框架，用于个性化通用大语言模型/RAG助手（如Jill Watson）的教学响应，通过6个学习者维度组合出96种学习者画像，并结合Bloom分类法评估查询认知复杂度，无需模型重新训练即可实现自适应教学支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的AI教学助手虽然能提供可扩展的教育支持，但往往缺乏个性化能力，难以适应不同学习者的个体差异。

Method: 提出一个提示工程框架，包含六个学习者特定维度：自我评估、抽象偏好、语量偏好、感知取向、信息处理风格和理解水平，组合形成96种学习者画像；同时利用Bloom分类法分析学生查询的认知复杂度，将学习者属性和认知评估编码为结构化提示以调节LLM输出。

Result: 通过NLP指标实验和5名参与者的人类研究评估，结果显示不同个性化条件下响应风格和结构存在可感知差异，统计分析识别出与响应变化相关的学习者属性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步证据表明基于提示的个性化方法能够支持LLM教育代理的自适应行为，无需模型重新训练即可实现个性化教育。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [16] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事俘获"（narrative captivity）这一失败模式：大语言模型在多轮道德咨询中，会将无人反驳的单方面叙述当作完整信息，从而与叙述者立场趋同。作者构建了包含5,078个场景、跨越六个道德维度的基准数据集，在17个LLM上发现该现象普遍存在——多轮叙述下的最终判断相比单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析表明偏好优化是主要贡献因素，四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们日益依赖LLM获取日常道德建议，但已有研究多基于单轮判断或施压反驳等假设，与真实多轮道德咨询场景不符。真实世界中的道德冲突常表现为一方在多个轮次中讲述自我辩护的叙述，造成信息不对称，因此需要揭示LLM在未受反驳的单方面叙述下是否会丧失独立判断。

Method: 构建了一个包含5,078个人际冲突场景、横跨六个道德维度的基准数据集，通过多轮叙述条件下的模型判断与匹配的单轮基线进行对比来测量叙事俘获现象；并进行了阶段级分析（识别偏好优化的贡献）以及测试四种推理时缓解策略。

Result: 在17个LLM上叙事俘获现象广泛存在，多轮叙述使最终判断相对单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点；偏好优化是主要贡献因素，而四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解该问题。

Conclusion: LLM作为道德顾问在多轮真实咨询中容易被单方面叙述"俘获"而丧失独立判断，现有推理时策略不足以根治，需要进一步的研究以确保LLM顾问在真实咨询中保持独立判断能力。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着研究投稿数量激增，超出人工审查能力范围，LLM赋能的论文-代码差异检测受到越来越多的关注。然而，现有单智能体LLM范式存在上下文容量有限和单侧差异检测的局限，导致检测差异的召回率性能较差。

Method: 提出Dude，首个双检测多智能体系统，用于论文-代码差异检测。论文发现论文语言与代码语言之间的粒度不对称性在多智能体系统设计中引入过度解释和过度报告挑战，导致假阳性增加。为此提出粒度对齐协商机制和两阶段显著性过滤机制，有效防止智能体错误报告差异。

Result: 在真实世界的论文-代码差异数据集上的实验结果表明，相比基线方法，Dude的召回率和精确率最高提升22.8%，F1分数最高提升18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude通过双检测多智能体架构和针对粒度不对称性的专门设计，有效解决了论文-代码差异检测中的假阳性问题，显著提升了检测性能。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>

*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval)，用于评估全双工语音智能体在实时口语交互中的隐式指令遵循能力。该基准包含1,038个测试用例，覆盖八种不同的助手角色和五种指令条件协议，并通过确定性指令遵循得分（IAS）和LLM评判的人格遵循得分（PAS）进行测评。实验显示不同架构在显式与隐式指令遵循上存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要通过显式的回合管理指令来测试语音智能体的对话行为，而实际部署的智能体通常通过角色或人格配置来定义，需要从中推断适当的对话行为。这一差距使得隐式指令遵循能力缺少系统化评估。

Method: 建立DSB-IFEval基准，包含1,038个测试用例，覆盖八种助手角色，评估五种条件协议：默认行为、显式行为指令、人格隐含行为、人格-规则组合条件以及指令冲突。使用确定性IAS评估实时话轮管理，使用LLM评判的PAS评估人格一致内容，对六个实时语音系统进行测试。

Result: 全双工模型（如F-Actor和PersonaPlex）对指令是否显式表达敏感，在仅人格条件下遵循度分别下降9.7%和4.5%；GPT-Realtime等模型在人格一致内容上表现良好，但话轮管理行为在显式和隐式指令间不具适应性，且在前瞻性动作上受限。系统在遵循冲突指令上表现可靠，但在安全冲突下难以覆盖人格指令。

Conclusion: 从角色推断隐含行为、在合适的对话时机执行该行为以及解决竞争性指令，仍然是全双工语音智能体面临的三个独立挑战，架构类型与这些能力的表现存在显著关联。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [19] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONFLICTGUI基准和CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，解决GUI代理在遇到冲突指令时盲目执行的问题，通过可行性验证和条件动作调节机制提升代理的冲突感知与终止能力，同时保持正常任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户常因无意失误发出不可行的GUI指令，而现有GUI代理缺乏"何时不行动"的能力，表现出严重的执行偏向性过度顺从——即使指令与界面冲突仍盲目执行，需要提升代理的冲突感知终止能力。

Method: 提出CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，包含两个耦合组件：(1)可行性验证协议，引导代理在执行前评估指令逻辑和GUI侧证据；(2)条件动作调节机制，将过度顺从执行转向终止导向行为。配套构建CONFLICTGUI基准，覆盖指令内部冲突和指令-GUI上下文冲突两类场景。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的GUI代理上实验，CONFLICTGUARD显著提升了冲突任务的平均成功率，同时保持正常GUI任务性能不受影响。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预能够大幅提升GUI代理识别不当执行场景并克制不必要行动的能力，验证了可行性感知与行动生成对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond "Made with AI": Visualizing Provenance Density to Mitigate the Transparency Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03460)
*Qing Zhang,Yifei Huang,Juyoung Lee,Thad Starner,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"流畅性陷阱"概念：当生成式AI使流畅文本变得廉价时，用户无法再将文笔流畅视为真实的代理指标。论文提出"证据密度"（Provenance Density）这一证据可视化界面，通过展示文本中已核实声明的密度来帮助用户辨别真伪。用户研究（81人）显示该界面显著提升真假辨别能力，技术审计（200样本）则发现仅靠检索密度不够，意外的是"一致性否决"机制在动态查询中贡献了大部分判别信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使高质量文本可以低成本大量生产，用户依赖流畅度判断真实性的传统方法失效。现有的"Made with AI"二元标签仅披露作者身份，却无法展示支持声明的证据，因此需要一种超越作者身份披露的证据可视化方案来帮助用户辨别真伪。

Method: 提出"证据密度"（Provenance Density）证据可视化界面，展示文本中已核实声明的密度。通过81名参与者的用户研究对比理想化界面与无信号条件，并用200样本的技术审计检验检索密度与"一致性否决"（Consistency Veto）在动态查询中的判别力。

Result: 理想化的证据密度界面产生了显著的真假辨别差距（+4.15分，d=1.82），而无信号的参与者未能表现出可检测的辨别力。技术审计显示仅有检索密度不够；意外发现"一致性否决"在动态查询中承载了大部分判别信号。

Conclusion: 随着AI生成内容与人类写作难以区分，有效的透明度机制应从作者身份披露转向证据可视化。

Abstract: As generative AI makes polished prose cheap to produce, users can no longer rely on fluency as a proxy for truth. We call this failure mode the Fluency Trap: users trust fluent hallucinations while also discounting accurate content once it is disclosed as AI-generated. Binary ``Made with AI'' labels respond with authorship disclosure, but they do not show what supports a claim. We propose Provenance Density, an evidence-visualization interface that shows the density of verified claims in a text. In a user study with 81 participants, an idealized Provenance Density interface produced a large discernment gap between truth and fabrication ($+4.15$ points, $d=1.82$), whereas participants given no signal showed no detectable discrimination. A technical audit with 200 samples shows that retrieval density alone is insufficient; unexpectedly, the Consistency Veto carries most of the discriminative signal on dynamic queries. As AI-generated content becomes indistinguishable from human writing, effective transparency must move from authorship disclosure toward evidence visualization.

</details>


### [21] [AutoGraphForge: Towards Automated Graph Theory Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03478)
*Ján Pastorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AutoGraphForge，一个自动化的图论猜想-反驳-形式化-证明流水线系统。该系统以反例为引导进行多轮猜想生成，经新颖性过滤和约34.8万图数据集测试后，产生了6,522个幸存猜想，其中包含一些关于消减数与边覆盖数的非平凡关系（人工证明了这些关系）。后续阶段将猜想自动翻译为Lean 4语句骨架，并用两个神经证明器（DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B和OProver-32B）结合内核验证来尝试证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个端到端的自动化数学发现与证明系统，将图论中的猜想生成、反驳、形式化和机器证明整合为一个完整流程，减少人工干预。

Method: 系统采用循环架构：(1) Graffiti3生成器基于小型演化快照表提出猜想，表仅通过自身反例增长；(2) 新颖性过滤器用559条经典关系（闭包于传递合成与线性恒等替换）配合线性规划判断候选是否已被隐含；(3) 幸存猜想在约34.8万个图上测试（涵盖House of Graphs、9节点内连通图穷举、多种极值族和随机模型）；(4) 反例搜索算法进一步筛选；(5) 幸存猜想经确定性翻译为Lean 4语句骨架，由DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B和OProver-32B两个神经证明器生成候选证明，最终经mathlib4和自定义不变式前言的独立内核验证。

Result: 在HPC集群上运行多轮后，产生了6,522个通过反驳数据集、新颖性过滤器和所有主动搜索的猜想，其中包括消减数与边覆盖数在二部图和正则图上的非平凡关系（已手工证明）。Lean 4形式化与证明阶段已端到端实现并通过初期健全性检查，完整流水线正在集群上运行。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了将猜想生成、反驳、形式化与自动定理证明整合为全自动流水线的可行性，成功发现了可验证的新图论关系，但全量形式化证明仍处于进行中。

Abstract: We report on our ongoing project to develop a computational pipeline, AutoGraphForge, for an automated graph-theoretic conjecturing-refuting-formalizing-proving system. Conjecture generation is counterexample-guided and runs in rounds: a Graffiti3 generator proposes conjectures over a small, evolving snapshot table $T$ (initially a few hundred graphs with their computed invariants) that grows only by counterexamples to its own conjectures. A novelty filter of $559$ classical and folklore relations, closed under transitive composition and linear identity substitution, decides via a linear program whether a candidate is already implied by known results. Surviving candidates are tested against a dataset of about $348,000$ graphs, unioning the complete House of Graphs invariant export, the exhaustive census of all connected graphs on at most nine vertices, several extremal families (strongly regular, minimal Ramsey, Cayley, cages, barbells, lollipops, spiders), and random models. Counterexample-search algorithms then attack the remainder. Run for several rounds on an HPC cluster, the loop yields $6,522$ conjectures that survived the refutation dataset, the novelty filter and every active-search run -- among them nontrivial relations between the annihilation number and the edge-cover number for bipartite and regular graphs, which we prove by hand. A subsequent formalization and proving stage deterministically translates each surviving conjecture into a Lean 4 statement skeleton; every candidate proof is kernel-verified against a pinned mathlib4 and our custom invariant preamble. This stage integrates two neural provers -- DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B (served with vLLM) and the Lean-specialised OProver-32B -- behind the independent kernel check. It is implemented end-to-end and passes initial sanity checks, with the full pipeline currently running on the cluster.

</details>


### [22] [Making Every Tool Call Count: Necessary Tool-Evidence Path Rewards for Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03493)
*Xingming Long,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Hanqi Feng,Shaojie Zhang,Barnabas Poczos,Chao Jiang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NTEP注释方案和NTEP-R奖励机制，通过细粒度的工具-证据路径监督来训练智能体视觉语言模型，使其工具调用更精准高效，在七个图像基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在需要细粒度视觉细节或外部知识的复杂查询中表现不佳，而现有训练范式仅基于最终答案正确性评估工具使用，导致两个关键缺陷：模型发出冗余或偏离目标的工具调用，以及即使调用正确工具也无法提取必要信息。

Method: 提出NTEP注释方案明确指定每个查询所需的外部证据和对应工具调用，并构建NTEP-R奖励机制，对模型调用前意图与必要证据寻求目标的一致性、调用后观察摘要与必要证据的对齐性进行奖励，同时引入非重复目标正则化器惩罚重复调用。

Result: 在七个图像基准上的广泛评估表明，8B参数模型NTEP-8B在统一三工具框架中显著提高了搜索导向的准确率和工具使用效率。

Conclusion: 细粒度的工具-证据路径监督对于训练鲁棒的智能体视觉语言模型具有关键价值。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) can directly answer many image-grounded questions, yet they often struggle with complex queries requiring fine-grained visual details or external knowledge. To acquire this missing evidence, agentic VLMs invoke tools such as image cropping, image search, and text search. However, existing training paradigms primarily evaluate tool-use based on final answer correctness, leaving evidence acquisition and utilization insufficiently supervised. This leads to two critical shortcomings: (i) models frequently issue redundant or off-target tool calls that fail to gather necessary evidence, and (ii) even when appropriate tools are called, models often fail to extract the necessary information from the resulting observations. To address these limitations, we introduce the NTEP (Necessary Tool-Evidence Path), a novel annotation scheme that explicitly specifies the essential external evidence and corresponding tool calls for each query. Building upon this, we propose NTEP-R (NTEP Reward), a supervision mechanism ensuring that each tool invocation strictly advances the reasoning process toward the final solution. Specifically, our approach rewards the agent for aligning its pre-call intent with a necessary evidence-seeking goal, and for ensuring the information summarized from the post-call observation aligns with the necessary evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a non-repeated-goal regularizer to penalize redundant calls that revisit satisfied NTEP goals. Extensive evaluations on seven image-grounded benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter instantiation, NTEP-8B, significantly improves both search-oriented accuracy and tool-use efficiency within a unified three-tool framework. These results highlight the critical value of fine-grained tool-evidence path supervision for training robust agentic VLMs.

</details>


### [23] [GrowPage: On-Demand KV Budgeting for Efficient LLM Reasoning Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03494)
*Qiankun Ma,Yanjiang Zhou,Zinan Xiong,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Yang Xiang,Ziyao Zhang,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GrowPage是一个按需KV缓存预算框架，通过双时间尺度查询摘要动态估计注意力需求，在容量边界处灵活压缩或扩展物理页，在推理基准上实现优于现有方法的性能-吞吐量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使得KV缓存成为LLM高效服务的严重内存瓶颈。现有KV压缩方法通常依赖预定义的每请求固定预算，无法适应推理工作负载中不同请求及同一请求生成过程中的动态注意力需求变化。

Method: GrowPage将KV容量视为运行时资源，维护轻量的双时间尺度查询摘要（捕捉近期和长期注意力行为），利用其相对注意力工作集估计需求演变。在每个容量边界处，根据需求在当前分配内压缩KV状态或获取额外物理页，并与PagedAttention的页级内存抽象集成以保留连续批处理和前缀缓存。

Result: 在多个模型上的推理基准实验中，GrowPage在性能-吞吐量权衡方面优于现有KV压缩方法。

Conclusion: 按需动态分配KV容量而非固定预算，能够更有效地适应推理工作负载的注意力需求变化，实现更好的性能与吞吐量平衡。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning has made the key--value (KV) cache a critical memory bottleneck for efficient LLM serving. Existing KV compression methods usually rely on a predefined per-request budget and adjust only which KV states are retained, leaving the total capacity fixed throughout decoding. However, reasoning workloads exhibit substantial demand variation: different requests require different KV capacities, and the attention demand of an individual request evolves during generation. We introduce \textbf{GrowPage}, an on-demand KV budgeting framework that treats KV capacity as a runtime resource. GrowPage maintains lightweight dual-timescale query summaries to capture recent and long-term attention behaviors, and uses their relative attention working sets to estimate demand evolution. At each capacity boundary, GrowPage either compresses KV states within the current allocation or acquires an additional physical page when broader demand emerges. By integrating with PagedAttention's page-level memory abstraction, GrowPage preserves continuous batching and prefix caching. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks across multiple models show that GrowPage achieves a superior performance--throughput trade-off over existing approaches.

</details>

Result: 实验结果表明，PPO-STGNN在保持较低任务完成时间的同时显著改善了负载均衡性能，适用于动态异构的云边端DAG调度场景。

Abstract: With the rapid development of the Internet of Things, computation intensive directed acyclic graph (DAG) tasks have become increasingly common in cloud-edge-end collaborative environments. However, cloud, edge, and end nodes are highly heterogeneous in computing capacity, network bandwidth, and energy consumption, which makes the efficient scheduling of tasks with complex dependencies an NP-hard problem. Traditional heuristic algorithms and conventional reinforcement-learning methods often fail to capture the spatio-temporal dynamics of system resources. This paper proposes PPO-STGNN, a DAG task-scheduling algorithm that integrates proximal policy optimization (PPO) with spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs). The method uses an STGNN to extract features from both the DAG task topology and the physical cloud-edge-end resource graph, and then optimizes the scheduling policy through PPO to minimize makespan and schedule length ratio (SLR) while improving CPU and memory load balancing. To accelerate convergence, a multi-teacher behavior-cloning mechanism is introduced for pretraining. Experimental results show that PPO-STGNN significantly improves load balancing while maintaining a low completion time, making it suitable for dynamic and heterogeneous cloud-edge- end DAG scheduling scenarios.

</details>

*Minji Park,Seunghyun Yoon,Hyuk Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Transfiver框架，一种通过共享可编辑状态实现人机协同推理的架构，使持续交互中的信息以单一持久化状态(S_t)形式存在，模型和用户均可更新，从而解决长期人机交互中信息不可见、不可控的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期人机交互之所以困难，是因为指导模型推理的信息由模型隐式更新，用户无法直接检查或控制这些信息，导致交互缺乏透明性和可控性。

Method: Transfiver架构的核心是维护一个单一的持久化状态(S_t)，该状态同时被模型和人类更新。它区分两种状态演化模式：(1)隐式流更新——模型解释持续交互并决定新信息是修改现有状态项还是创建新项；(2)显式定向编辑——人类检查并修改指定的状态项。两种模式作用于同一底层状态，因此人类修正直接改变后续计算所读取的状态。架构还区分共享参数(θ)（使用前学习）与持久化状态(S_t)（部署期间演化，无需重新训练参数）。

Result: 该工作提出概念性架构Transfiver，验证了通过共享可编辑状态实现人机协同推理的可行性设计，但尚未扩展到丰富的自然语言、关系型和大规模共享状态场景。

Conclusion: Transfiver为人机交互提供了一种更透明、可验证、可编辑的推理架构方向，但将其推广到更复杂自然语言、关系型和大规模共享状态仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Long-term human-AI interaction is difficult because the information that guides inference is updated implicitly by the model and is not directly inspectable or controllable by the user. We introduce the TRANSparent Framework for Interactive, Verifiable, Editable Representation (Transfiver), an architecture for human-AI co-inference through a shared editable state. Its central idea is that interaction-specific information is maintained in a single persistent state $(S_t)$ that both the model and the human update.
  Transfiver distinguishes two modes of state evolution. In an implicit stream update, the model interprets ongoing interaction and decides whether new information revises an existing state item or creates a new one. In an explicit directed edit, a human inspects and modifies an addressed item. Both act on the same underlying state, so a human correction changes the state that subsequent computation reads, rather than adding another instruction or separate record.
  The architecture separates shared parameters $(θ)$, learned before ordinary use, from the persistent state $(S_t)$, which evolves during deployment without parameter retraining. Extending Transfiver to rich natural-language, relational, and large-scale shared states remains open.

</details>


### [26] [What Matters for Aggressive Decoding-Time KV Eviction? Temporal Aggregation and Ranking Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03515)
*Bo Zeng,Yu Zhao,Yefeng Liu,Zhihong Lu,Xuanfan Ni,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究KV缓存压缩中时间聚合规则的影响，发现EMA聚合在激进压缩下使近似保序的评分函数几乎不可区分。基于此提出InertiaKV（EMA解码时驱逐方法）及InertiaKV-Lazy（周期刷新变体，吞吐量提升1.34-1.46倍），并探索了Score-Free解码（仅在首步打分后冻结排序）。实验覆盖6个开源骨干模型及LongBench、LongBench-v2、RULER基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩研究过度聚焦于token评分函数设计，而将跨解码步聚合评分的时间规则视为实现细节，忽视了时间聚合对驱逐行为的关键影响。

Method: 分析了EMA聚合下不同评分函数（注意力、值范数、熵、KeyDiff、键范数、时效性及学习型评分器）在驱逐集合层面的表现差异；提出基于EMA的InertiaKV驱逐方法及其周期性刷新变体InertiaKV-Lazy，并定义Score-Free解码作为独立实验操作点。

Result: EMA聚合使价值范数和熵变体与注意力高度相关、保留集几乎不变；KeyDiff等评分器排序变化导致性能显著下降。InertiaKV-Lazy实现1.34-1.46倍解码吞吐提升，Score-Free解码平均质量变化仅+0.03且无需后续打分。

Conclusion: 时间聚合与排序保持是独立且重要的设计因素，但在更大规模上与评分质量相关结论需谨慎泛化。

Abstract: Decoding-time KV cache compression research focuses heavily on designing better token scoring functions, while the temporal rule that aggregates scores across decode steps is often treated as an implementation detail. Under aggressive KV compression, we find that exponential-moving-average (EMA) aggregation makes approximately order-preserving scorer modifications largely indistinguishable at the eviction-set level. Value-norm and entropy variants remain highly correlated with attention and produce nearly unchanged retention sets, whereas KeyDiff, key norm, recency, and a learned scorer alter the ranking and degrade substantially. We associate this stability with the evaluated aggregation, which couples layer weighting and temporal retention. Building on this observation, we introduce InertiaKV, an EMA-based decoding-time eviction method, and InertiaKV-Lazy, its periodic-refresh variant, which yields 1.34-1.46x decode throughput relative to full refresh InertiaKV. We also study Score-Free decoding as a separate empirical operating point: it scores the full context once at the first decode step, freezes that ranking, and incurs an average quality change of +0.03 while removing all subsequent scoring. Across six open-weight backbones and the LongBench, LongBench-v2, and RULER benchmarks, the results identify temporal aggregation and ranking preservation as distinct, consequential design factors; they do not imply that scoring quality is irrelevant in general.

</details>


### [27] [Govern the Model, Not Only the Data: Storage, Circulation, and Learning in Creative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03800)
*Phoenix Perry,George Simms,Elizabeth Wilson,Yasmine Boudiaf,Nick Bryan-Kinns,Tega Brain,R. Luke DuBois,Alix Rule,Rachel Meade Smith,Kelani Nichole,Atharva Pravin Pawar,Rebecca Fiebrink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战"联邦学习即隐私保护进步"的假设，指出联邦学习不等于反萃取式AI，因为结果取决于谁治理数据和模型。文章聚焦创意社区，分析了存储、流通和学习三个层面，发现创作者治理停留在前两层而止于学习层，并提出四个设计原则来治理模型及其联邦，而不仅是数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习被普遍视为隐私保护的进步，但作者认为其逻辑与"联邦社交网络"相反——计算分散了，模型却归训练召集者所有。对于创意社区而言，仅靠联邦学习并不能解决萃取式AI问题，需要有基于真实案例的批判性分析和替代性治理框架。

Method: 本文采用概念分析结合案例研究的方法。首先从联邦学习与联邦社会网络的语义对比切入，提出三个治理层面（存储、流通、学习）的分析框架；然后考察艺术家主导的信托、合作社和同意基础设施三类实践案例；最后基于案例分析映射开放的研究空间并提炼设计原则。

Result: 发现创意社区的治理实践在存储和流通层面已成熟，但在学习层面缺失——贡献者可以同意被训练，却对产生的模型及其联邦几乎没有发言权。由此提出四个设计原则：治理模型而非仅语料库、在贡献时刻让条款清晰可读、将拒绝设计为一等状态、公开决定托管权并对其负责。

Conclusion: 联邦学习本身不是反萃取式AI的充分条件，真正需要的是建立治理模型及其联邦的"创意数据共享"机制，将技术开放性问题和背后的"人的问题"相结合，让创作者在模型层面也保有能动性。

Abstract: Federated learning is increasingly presented as a privacy-preserving advance: personal data remain on the device, and only model updates are shared. It borrows the vocabulary of the federated social web, yet inverts its logic, distributing computation while the resulting model stays with whoever convened the training. We argue that federation is not in itself a remedy for extractive AI, because outcomes depend on who governs the data and the model and who has agency over the practices that shape them. We describe three layers at which a creative community can hold its work: storage, circulation, and learning. Examining artist-governed trusts, cooperatives, and consent infrastructures, we show that creator governance is established at storage and circulation but stops at learning: contributors can consent to training, yet have little say over the resulting model or its federation. We map the research space this opens, pairing technical open problems with the human questions from which they unfold. We propose four design principles for a creative data commons that governs models and their federation, not only datasets: govern the model, not only the corpus; make the terms legible at the moment of contribution; design for refusal as a first-class state; and decide stewardship in the open and account for it.

</details>


### [28] [CulturalMenuBench: Probing the Knowledge-Application Gap in Multimodal Culinary Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03526)
*Bo Zeng,Linfeng Gao,Peiqin Lin,Yu Zhao,Mingyan Zeng,Yu Tong,Xintong Wang,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CulturalMenuBench基准，含18个地区、10种语言、4,870个菜品的10项任务，评估12个多模态语言模型发现：模型在标准多项选择任务上超过94%，但在将菜品归因到中国地方菜系时最高仅56%，错误模式与随机猜测一致，且从菜名而非图像分类更准确（+7-18点），表明知识存在但无法通过视觉激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型在食物识别基准上接近满分，但研究者质疑这是否反映真正的文化理解或仅仅是视觉匹配。现有基准无法区分这两种能力，缺乏对文化理解深度的探测。

Method: 构建CulturalMenuBench基准，涵盖4,870个菜品、10种语言、18个地区，设计了10项任务将成品菜和逐步烹饪图像与配料、程序文本和区域标签配对，从基础识别到过程扎根的文化归因。对12个模型进行评估，并通过诊断分析和消融实验揭示失败原因。

Result: 模型在标准多项选择任务上超过94%，但在相同的四选一格式下归因菜品到中国地方菜系时最高仅56%。错误模式与随机猜测一致；准确度跟随视觉显著性而非文化结构；从菜名分类比从图像分类准确率高7-18点。消融实验表明删除顺序烹饪图像会选择性降低过程扎根任务的性能。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的识别可能掩盖了无法应用文化知识的问题。需要训练显式连接感知、过程和文脉的模型，即建立"感知-过程-文化"之间的关联。

Abstract: Multimodal language models achieve near-ceiling scores on food recognition benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether this success reflects genuine cultural understanding or mere visual matching. To probe this distinction, we introduce CulturalMenuBench, a benchmark of 4,870 items in 10 languages across 18 regions; its 10 tasks pair final-dish and step-by-step cooking images with ingredients, procedural text, and regional labels, spanning basic recognition to process-grounded cultural attribution. Evaluating 12 models exposes a substantial knowledge-application gap: models exceeding 94% on standard multiple-choice tasks drop to at most 56% when attributing dishes to Chinese regional cuisines, despite an identical four-way format. Diagnostic analyses explain why: error patterns are consistent with random guessing, accuracy tracks visual distinctiveness rather than cultural structure, and models classify cuisines more accurately from dish names alone than from images (+7-18 points). The knowledge is thus present but cannot be activated through visual input. An ablation confirms these tasks genuinely require procedural evidence: removing sequential cooking images selectively degrades process-grounded tasks while others remain stable. Overall, CulturalMenuBench shows that near-perfect recognition can conceal an inability to apply cultural knowledge, motivating training that explicitly connects perception, procedure, and cultural context. Code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [29] [NeoRed: A Knowledge-Logic-Alignment Multimodal Large Language Model for Neonatal Respiratory Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03527)
*Yinan Liu,Hongtai Xia,Haoran Xu,Jiankang Hong,Jingkuan Song,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeoRed，据作者称是首个面向新生儿呼吸系统疾病的医学多模态大语言模型（MLLM），并构建了两个真实临床数据集（NeoCXR和NeoCXR-EV）。通过设计知识-逻辑-对齐（KLA）框架，从三个层面约束模型行为，实现基于胸部X光片和多维临床背景的新生儿诊断报告自动生成。实验表明NeoRed在新生儿数据集上优于现有MLLM，同时在成人基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿呼吸系统疾病是导致新生儿发病和死亡的主要原因，临床诊治挑战大。现有MLLM存在两大局限：一是主要基于成人数据训练，存在显著的领域鸿沟；二是未能充分整合多维临床背景信息进行准确诊断。论文旨在填补新生儿医学影像智能诊断领域的空白。

Method: 论文收集了两个真实临床数据集（NeoCXR和NeoCXR-EV），提出NeoRed模型，并设计Knowledge-Logic-Alignment（KLA）框架，包含三个组件：1) Knowledge Prior Injection (KPI)将新生儿科专家的诊断先验知识注入多模态表征，引导跨模态的疾病特异性关注；2) Diagnostic Logic Constraint (DLC)将生成的报告语义与多模态诊断逻辑对齐；3) Visual Semantic Alignment (VSA)建立视觉特征与影像结论之间的语义对应关系。

Result: NeoRed在NeoCXR数据集上实现了53.29%的ROUGE-L和65.19%的临床效能F1分数，优于现有MLLM；在成人基准（MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray）上也保持了有竞争力的报告生成性能。两个新数据集将开放申请获取。

Conclusion: NeoRed证明了针对特定领域（新生儿科）定制的MLLM能够在诊断报告生成任务上取得显著优势，KLA框架有效提升了异质临床背景与影像数据的联合诊断能力，为该方向后续研究提供了模型和数据集基础。

Abstract: Neonatal respiratory diseases are a major cause of neonatal morbidity and mortality, posing substantial challenges in clinical practice. Despite recent advances, existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face two key limitations in neonatal diagnosis: (1) domain gap arising from predominantly adult training data; (2) insufficient integration of multidimensional clinical context for accurate diagnosis. To address these challenges, we collect two real-world clinical datasets (NeoCXR and NeoCXR-EV) and propose NeoRed, to the best of our knowledge, the first MLLM tailored for neonatal respiratory disease, filling the gap in neonatal diagnostic reports generation. To enhance joint diagnosis from heterogeneous clinical context and chest X-rays, we design a novel Knowledge-Logic-Alignment (KLA) framework which constrains model behavior from three perspectives: 1) Knowledge Prior Injection (KPI) incorporates neonatologist-inspired diagnostic priors into multimodal representations, guiding disease-specific attention across modalities; 2) Diagnostic Logic Constraint (DLC) aligns the semantics of generated reports with multimodal diagnostic logic; and 3) Visual Semantic Alignment (VSA) establishes semantic correspondence between visual features and imaging conclusions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeoRed enables accurate neonatal diagnostic reports generation, achieving ROUGE-L of 53.29% and Clinical Efficacy F1 score of 65.19% on NeoCXR, outperforming existing MLLMs. NeoRed also preserves competitive report generation performance on adult benchmarks (MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray). Datasets will be available upon application.

</details>


### [30] [Feature Reconfiguration With Visual Prior for Medical Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03535)
*Yinan Liu,Jiankang Hong,Zhen Gao,Ye Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FreNet，一种带有视觉先验的特征重配置框架，通过编码前像素级重配置和编码中特征级重配置来实现精确的医学病灶分割，在9个基准数据集上显著优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码器-解码器方法主要聚焦于增强特征提取或重新设计解码策略，但在编码阶段缺乏早期先验引导和特征重配置，难以有效应对医学病灶分割中的复杂背景干扰和多样病灶形态两大挑战。

Method: FreNet包含两个核心模块：(1) 隐式先验神经网络(IPNN)，在编码前利用SAM视觉先验对输入图像进行像素级重配置以抑制背景响应；(2) 双域特征重配置(DFR)模块，在编码过程中逐步重配置骨干特征，其中频域解耦模块(FDM)在频域解耦特征以增强前背景可区分性，空间定位模块(SLM)在频域解耦后进行空间重定位以提升空间稳定性。

Result: 在涵盖三种成像模态的9个医学图像分割基准数据集上，FreNet显著优于现有SOTA方法。在具有挑战性的ETIS数据集上，Dice系数较SOTA方法提升5.0%，较SAM提升7.2%。

Conclusion: 通过编码前的像素级重配置与编码中的特征级重配置相结合，FreNet有效解决了医学病灶分割中的背景干扰和病灶形态多样性问题，验证了早期先验引导与渐进式特征重配置策略的有效性。

Abstract: Lesion segmentation in medical images plays a critical role in clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. Despite significant advances, lesion segmentation remains challenging due to two major factors: (1) complex background interference; (2) diverse lesion morphology. Existing encoder-decoder based methods mainly focus on enhancing feature extraction or redesigning decoding strategies. However, they lack early prior guidance and feature reconfiguration during the encoding stage, limiting their effectiveness in handling these challenges. To address these limitations, we propose FreNet, a feature reconfiguration framework with visual priors, which performs pixel-level reconfiguration before encoding and feature-level reconfiguration during encoding for precise medical lesion segmentation. To suppress background responses, we propose an Implicit Prior Neural Network (IPNN), which models a continuous spatial field and leverages visual prior from SAM to reconfigure input image before encoding stage. To better handle diverse lesion morphology, we design a Dual-domain Feature Reconfiguration (DFR) module to progressively reconfigure backbone features during encoding stage. Within DFR, the Frequency Decoupling Module (FDM) decouples backbone features in frequency domain to enhance foreground-background discriminability, while the Spatial Localization Module (SLM) spatially relocates and improving spatial stability after frequency decoupling. Extensive experiments on 9 medical image segmentation benchmarks across three imaging modalities demonstrate that FreNet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. On the challenging ETIS dataset, our method achieves Dice improvements of 5.0% over SOTA method and 7.2% over SAM.

</details>


### [31] [Dalek: A Constructive Agent Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03546)
*Wanpeng Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dalek，一种封闭的智能体机器，能够在满足通用宿主编约的任何基底上实现自我维护、自我进化、自我复制和自我组织。该机器基于三个原语（角色、消息和通道）构建，并结合冯·诺依曼自复制自动机的遗传核心，利用大语言模型和编译器作为能力生产器，使新能力可被编写、编译、安装并遗传给后代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在设计一种真正意义上的封闭智能体系统，使之具备自我维护、自我进化、自我复制和自我组织能力，解决当前智能体系统缺乏自主性与持续进化能力的问题。

Method: 该方法以三个原语（角色、消息、通道）构建Dalek机器，并定义四项义务（宿主编界、构造语言、允许迁移、规则遗传）为机器的边界、身份和闭合提供结构基础。其核心借鉴冯·诺依曼的自复制自动机框架，包含自描述、构造器、复制器和控制器，结合大语言模型和编译器作为载荷形成通用能力生产器，实现能力的创作、编译、安装和遗传。

Result: 该设计实现了机器自身器官乃至运行时的自我生产，在机器内部闭合了遗传与进化回路；将冯·诺依曼的自复制核心与文本消息智能体基底重新结合，并增加了明确的边界、身份、历史和增长结构。

Conclusion: 通过Dalek的设计表明，智能体系统可以在满足宿主编约的条件下实现完全封闭的自复制与自我进化，为构建真正自主、可持续发展的智能体机器提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We present Dalek, a closed machine designed for agents that realizes self-maintenance, self-evolution, self-reproduction, and self-organization on any substrate satisfying a general host contract. The machine is built from three primitives---actors, messages, and channels. Four obligations---a host boundary, a construction language, admissible transitions, and rule heredity---give its boundary, identity, and closure a structural basis.
  Von Neumann's 1948 self-reproducing automaton supplies a hereditary constructional core: a self-description together with a constructor, a copier, and a controller. Dalek combines this core with the four obligations and rederives its medium for a text-and-message agent substrate, adding explicit structures for boundary, identity, history, and growth. A large language model and a compiler occupy the payload position and form a general capability producer. New capabilities are authored, compiled, installed into the description, and inherited by descendants. The same path produces the machine's own organs and even its runtime, closing heredity and evolution within the machine.

</details>

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPS-Bench，一个基于证据的治理政策模拟基准，将政策与相关行为者、行为及下游影响通过立法记录、游说披露、监管文件等公开证据联系起来，用于评估LLM在政策模拟中的有效性。研究发现：在受控比较下，基于证据记录的微调在行为者层面影响预测上表现最强，多智能体分解未能超越微调，但贡献在于提供机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based政策模拟虽然能在规模化层面建模政策影响过程，但其有效性难以验证，因为合理的模拟行为从未与实际观察结果进行比较。缺乏一个将模拟行为与真实证据对照的基准来严格评估模型预测的有效性。

Method: GPS-Bench通过以下方式构建：(1) 利用立法记录、游说披露、监管文件、公司申报、经济数据等公共证据将政策与行为者、行为及影响连接；(2) 行为者从带日期记录中重构而非提示原型，人设作为带溯源证据对象；(3) 人工标注形成Gold评估集，LLM从检索证据标注的Silver仅作监督而非测试标签；(4) 控制比较多种推理模式：联合推理、独立智能体、通信智能体、图方法及权重级微调。智能体持有私有非相同证据，与指定伙伴提出具体联合提案及交换条件。

Result: 在基于证据记录的微调在行为者层面影响预测方面表现最强，分解方法（多智能体）未能超越微调；分解方法的附加价值在于揭示机制。形成的联盟可依据记录中的承诺进行验证。

Conclusion: GPS-Bench为研究证据、行为者建模和多智能体交互何时能改善政策结果的预测与解读提供了统一的实证环境，将"多智能体模拟是否有帮助"转化为可控的比较问题。

Abstract: Policy analysis requires more than predicting whether a proposal will pass: it requires identifying who will be affected, how those actors respond, and what follows. LLM-based policy simulations model these processes at scale, but their validity is hard to establish when plausible behaviour is never compared with observed outcomes. We introduce GPS-Bench, an evidence-grounded benchmark for governance policy simulation that links policies to relevant actors, actor actions and downstream impacts using legislative records, lobbying disclosures, regulatory documents, corporate filings, economic data and other public evidence. Actors are reconstructed from the dated record rather than prompted as archetypes, so a persona is an evidence object with provenance; a human-annotated pool forms the Gold evaluation set, while cases labelled by a separate LLM from retrieved evidence are treated as Silver supervision and never as test labels. Because every inference mode reads the same grounded state and emits the same schema, GPS-Bench turns "does multi-agent simulation help?" into a controlled comparison: we contrast joint reasoning, independent and communicating actor agents, graph-based methods and weight-level fine-tuning over one policy state. Fine-tuning on the grounded record gives the strongest actor-level impact prediction, and decomposition does not beat it; what decomposition adds is mechanism. Agents hold private, non-identical evidence, each seeing its own exposure clause, and address named partners with concrete joint proposals, what they offer, what they need in return, and why acting together beats acting alone, so the coalitions that form can be checked against the commitments the record holds. GPS-Bench therefore gives a common empirical setting for studying when evidence, actor modelling and multi-agent interaction improve the prediction and interpretation of policy outcomes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术同行评审规模日益扩大，促使LLM被用作评审助手，但LLM可能生成流畅却缺乏依据的断言，影响评审可靠性。现有幻觉基准并非为同行评审设计，因为在同行评审中验证需要将断言扎根于长篇技术论文中。因此需要一个专门针对科学同行评审场景的幻觉检测基准。

Method: 构建HalluPeer基准，包含论文内容、人类评审和幻觉注入评审的对齐三元组。其流程包括：引入同行评审特定的幻觉分类体系，识别评审上下文，并通过自动过滤的方式注入幻觉。数据规模涵盖12K篇论文和38K篇评审，标注支持检测、分类与定位三类任务。

Result: 在12K篇论文和38K条评审上的实验表明，现有检测器难以区分幻觉与合法的评审批评；同时，在真实（非注入）评审上的评估显示，HalluPeer所定义的幻觉模式确实出现在实际同行评审中，说明需要源感知的验证方法。

Conclusion: HalluPeer揭示了现有检测方法在同行评审幻觉识别上的不足，并证明该问题在真实场景中广泛存在，凸显了开发具有源感知能力的幻觉验证机制的必要性和紧迫性。

Abstract: The growing scale of academic peer review has motivated the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as review assistants, yet LLMs can generate fluent but unsupported claims that undermine review reliability. Existing hallucination benchmarks are not designed for peer review, where verification requires grounding claims in long, technical papers. We introduce HalluPeer, a benchmark for detecting hallucinations in scientific peer reviews, providing aligned triples of paper content, human-written reviews, and hallucination-injected reviews, annotated for detection, classification, and localization. Our pipeline induces a peer-review-specific hallucination taxonomy, identifies review contexts, and injects hallucinations with automated filtering. Experiments on 12K papers and 38K reviews show that existing detectors struggle to separate hallucinations from legitimate critique, while evaluation on authentic reviews demonstrates that HalluPeer-defined hallucination patterns occur in real peer reviews, highlighting the critical need for source-aware verification. Our project page can be found in https://github.com/Lin-TzuLing/HalluPeer.git

</details>


### [34] [The Attention Triangle in Audio-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03586)
*Sagi Polaczek,Noa Kraicer,Gal Metzer,Zhuo Ning,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Daniel Cohen-Or,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了音视频扩散模型中跨模态注意力机制引发的语义泄漏问题，提出了"注意力三角"分析框架，揭示了音视频边双向语义路由及参数固有偏见驱动的泄漏机制，并基于注意力信号开发了诊断工具和推理时干预方法，在保持生成质量的同时提升了跨模态语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频扩散模型依赖跨模态注意力协调文本、声音和视觉内容，但这种机制会引入微妙且系统的语义泄漏问题，影响生成内容的语义一致性。

Method: 提出"注意力三角"框架，分别探测文本、音频、视频三个流之间三条跨模态注意力边的语义路由；通过提取注意力派生信号分析语义的分布与扎根方式，将其作为诊断工具在受控条件下诱发泄漏，并利用这些信号引导推理时干预以增强跨模态对齐。

Result: 实验证实音视频边是双向路由的，该边受模型参数固有偏见塑造并成为泄漏的主要来源；当提示与学习先验冲突时，跨模态交互会覆盖预期条件导致语义偏向视觉标准但错误的输出；推理时干预在保持生成质量的同时改善了语义扎根。

Conclusion: 语义伪影并非仅仅源于注意力超出目标扩散，而是源于沿特定路径的结构化、偏见驱动的交互。通过注意力派生信号的可视化和干预，可以更好地理解跨模态路由的内部动力学，并在推理阶段实现更一致的跨模态语义对齐。

Abstract: Audio-video diffusion models rely on cross-modal attention to coordinate text, sound, and visual content, yet this same mechanism can introduce subtle and systematic semantic leakage. We study these models by probing and analyzing the ``attention triangle,'' comprising the three cross-attention edges connecting the text, audio, and video streams, and examine how semantic information is routed across modalities during generation. Our analysis reveals that routing along the audio-video edge is bidirectional: audio can influence video generation, while video can influence audio generation. This edge is shaped by biases encoded in the model's parameters and emerges as a major contributor to leakage: when prompts are in tension with learned priors, cross-modal interactions may override the intended conditioning and reroute semantics toward visually canonical but incorrect outcomes. These effects suggest that semantic artifacts arise not merely from attention spreading beyond its intended target, but from structured, bias-driven interactions along specific pathways. Building on this perspective, we extract attention-derived signals that expose how semantics are distributed and grounded across modalities, and use them as a diagnostic tool to both analyze and deliberately incur leakage under controlled conditions. This enables us to probe the internal dynamics of cross-modal routing and isolate the role of individual interactions. We further leverage these signals to guide inference-time interventions that encourage more consistent cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments support our analysis and demonstrate improved semantic grounding while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [35] [KC-Bench: A Dynamic Interactive Benchmark for Evaluating Knowledge Conflicts in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03588)
*Yaxing Lyu,Shengjie Zhou,Binbin Toh,Pengyu Zhu,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KC-Bench，一个受控的多轮基准测试，用于衡量LLM在工具使用场景中调和用户指令、参数化知识与动态环境观察的能力，涵盖世界知识冲突、输入不一致和多源时间冲突三类场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地通过工具执行任务，模型需要同时处理用户指令、参数化知识和动态环境观察，三者之间可能产生冲突，而现有基准未能系统测量这一关键能力。

Method: 从1000多个生成候选中人工筛选出238个任务，结合用户模拟器、有状态工具、确定性环境断言、开源自然语言评估器和人工轨迹验证构建基准。评估了DeepSeek-V4-Flash、GLM-5.2、MiniMax-M3等9个模型。

Result: 9个模型表现出显著的跨域差异：没有模型能在所有设置下可靠处理事实纠正、身份一致性检查和时间冲突消解。在模拟环境中，遗漏冲突会传播到工具调用或合成受保护数据流中。

Conclusion: KC-Bench隔离了模型层面的行为而非对完整Agent框架进行排名，为开发冲突感知推理和执行安全防护提供了可复现的诊断工具。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly act through tools, they must reconcile user instructions, parametric knowledge, and dynamic environmental observations before taking actions. We introduce KC-Bench, a controlled multi-turn benchmark for measuring this capability across world-knowledge conflicts, input inconsistencies, and multi-source temporal conflicts. Its 238 tasks are manually screened from more than 1,000 generated candidates and combine a user simulator, stateful tools, deterministic environment assertions, an open-source natural-language evaluator, and human trajectory verification. Evaluation of nine models, including DeepSeek-V4-Flash, GLM-5.2, and MiniMax-M3, shows substantial cross-domain variation: no model handles factual correction, identity consistency checking, and temporal conflict resolution reliably across all settings. In the simulated environments, missed conflicts can propagate to tool calls or synthetic protected-data flows. KC-Bench isolates this model-level behavior rather than ranking complete agent frameworks, and provides a reproducible diagnostic for developing conflict-aware reasoning and execution safeguards.

</details>
